# Master Program Files and Functions Inventory

> Comprehensive audit of every program file, class, method, CLI command, and GUI available function in Cortex Cleaner.
> Total Program Files Audited: **492**
> Total Registered GUI Pages: **139**

## Executive Summary of Codebase Architecture
- **User Interface Layer**: Premium Qt6 Shell (`src/cortex_unified/ui/premium/`) with **139** registered modular tool pages, dark-mode styling, worker multithreading, and `StatePanel` UX patterns.
- **Command Line Interface Layer**: Click CLI (`src/cortex_unified/cli/cli.py` & `src/cortex_unified/engine/cli.py`) offering headless scriptable automation.
- **System Engine Layer**: Deep Windows analyzers, forensic cleaner tools, DISM components, network gateway UPnP auditors, memory standbylist purgers, and duplicate detection suites.
- **Nexus Explorer Native Layer**: Windows shell extensions, thumbnail providers, and context menu handlers.

---

### File: `tests/test_config_legacy_loading.py`

  - `def test_empty_file_is_treated_as_no_settings(tmp_path, caplog)` (Line 60) - An empty file parses to None; that is defaults, not an error.
  - `def test_non_utf8_bytes_warn_and_fall_back(tmp_path, caplog)` (Line 70) - Explicit UTF-8 decoding means bad bytes are reported, not locale-luck.
  - `def test_unicode_paths_load_correctly(tmp_path)` (Line 81) - Non-ASCII config content must load regardless of system locale.
  - `def test_protected_directories_are_excluded_by_default(tmp_path, name)` (Line 100) - With no config file, the safety exclusions must still apply.

  - `def test_defaults_still_apply_when_the_file_is_broken(tmp_path, caplog)` (Line 117) - A malformed file must not silently drop the safety exclusions.
  - `def test_user_settings_override_defaults_key_by_key(tmp_path)` (Line 127) - An explicit list replaces the default; untouched keys are inherited.
  - `def test_config_data_is_mutable_without_corrupting_other_instances(tmp_path)` (Line 140) - The CLI applies overrides by assigning into ``config_data``.
